
# from .models import Scheduler, DayPlan, Task

import random
import json
from django.shortcuts import render, redirect
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.contrib.auth import authenticate, login, logout
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required, user_passes_test
from django.contrib import messages
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.views.decorators.csrf import csrf_exempt
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# AUTH VIEWS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def home(request):
    return render(request, 'home.html')


def register_view(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        username = request.POST['username']
        password = request.POST['password']

        if User.objects.filter(username=username).exists():
            messages.error(request, "Username already exists")
            return redirect('register')

        user = User.objects.create_user(username=username, password=password)
        login(request, user)
        return redirect('dashboard')

    return render(request, 'register.html')


def login_view(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        username = request.POST['username']
        password = request.POST['password']
        user = authenticate(request, username=username, password=password)

        if user is not None:
            login(request, user)
            return redirect('dashboard')
        else:
            messages.error(request, "Invalid credentials")

    return render(request, 'login.html')


def logout_view(request):
    logout(request)
    return redirect('home')


@login_required
def dashboard(request):
    return render(request, 'dashboard.html')


def admin_check(user):
    return user.is_superuser


@login_required
@user_passes_test(admin_check)
def admin_page(request):
    return render(request, 'admin_page.html')


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# SCHEDULER VIEWS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

from django.shortcuts import render

def scheduler(request):
    return render(request, "scheduler.html")


@csrf_exempt
@require_POST
def generate_plan_view(request):
    """
    POST /generate-plan/
    Body (JSON): { "total_days": <int> }
    Returns JSON with study_plan + revision_plan
    """
    try:
        body = json.loads(request.body)
        total_days = int(body.get("total_days", 0))
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError, TypeError):
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Invalid request body. Send JSON with total_days."}, status=400)

    if total_days < 1:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "total_days must be a positive integer."}, status=400)

    plan = generate_plan(allProblems, total_days)
    revision = create_revision_sheet(plan, total_days)

    return JsonResponse({
        "total_days": total_days,
        "study_plan": plan,
        "revision_plan": revision,
    })

import random


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# REFERENCE LINKS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def get_reference_links(topic, max_links=3):
    links = REFERENCE_LINKS.get(topic, [])
    return links[:max_links] if links else []


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# PLAN ENGINE HELPERS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def build_topic_plan(total_days):

    if total_days <= 25:
        topic_order = [
            "Arrays", "Hashing", "Two Pointers", "Sliding Window",
            "Bit Manipulation", "Linked List", "Stack", "Queue",
            "Strings", "Sorting & Searching", "Trees", "Graphs",
            "Dynamic Programming", "Kadane's Algorithm",
        ]

        topic_map = {
            "Arrays": ["Array Manipulation", "Prefix Sum", "Prefix Product", "Matrix", "Math"],
            "Hashing": ["Hashing"],
            "Two Pointers": ["Two Pointers"],
            "Sliding Window": ["Sliding Window"],
            "Bit Manipulation": ["Bit Manipulation", "Boyer-Moore Voting"],
            "Linked List": ["Linked List"],
            "Stack": ["Stack"],
            "Queue": [],
            "Strings": ["String Manipulation", "String Matching"],
            "Sorting & Searching": ["Sorting", "Merge Sort", "Quickselect", "Binary Search", "Linear Search"],
            "Trees": ["Binary Tree", "Binary Search Tree", "AVL Tree", "Trie", "Segment Tree", "Fenwick Tree", "Heap"],
            "Graphs": ["Graph", "BFS", "DFS", "Dijkstra", "Shortest Path",
                       "Minimum Spanning Tree", "Topological Sort",
                       "Union Find", "Disjoint Set", "Graph Coloring"],
            "Dynamic Programming": ["Dynamic Programming", "Divide and Conquer", "Greedy", "Backtracking"],
            "Kadane's Algorithm": ["Kadane's Algorithm"],
        }
    else:
        topic_order = RAW_TOPICS
        topic_map = None

    return topic_order, topic_map


def get_links_per_day(days):
    if days <= 15:
        return 25
    if days <= 30:
        return 15
    if days <= 59:
        return 10
    if days <= 100:
        return 8
    return 5


def group_by_topic_and_difficulty(problems):
    grouped = {}
    for p in problems:
        topic = p.get("topic")
        difficulty = p.get("difficulty")

        if topic not in grouped:
            grouped[topic] = {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []}

        if difficulty in grouped[topic]:
            grouped[topic][difficulty].append(p)

    return grouped


def compute_dynamic_topic_weight(topic_order, grouped):
    weight = {}
    for t in topic_order:
        if t not in grouped:
            weight[t] = 1
        else:
            total = sum(len(grouped[t][lvl]) for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"])
            weight[t] = max(1, total)
    return weight


def calculate_topic_days_accurate(total_days, topic_order, grouped, links_per_day):

    capacity = {}

    for t in topic_order:
        if t not in grouped:
            capacity[t] = 1
        else:
            total_questions = sum(len(grouped[t][lvl]) for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"])
            capacity[t] = max(1, (total_questions + links_per_day - 1) // links_per_day)

    weight = compute_dynamic_topic_weight(topic_order, grouped)
    total_weight = sum(weight.values())

    days_map = {}
    used_days = 0

    for t in topic_order:
        proportional_days = max(1, int(weight[t] / total_weight * total_days))
        proportional_days = min(proportional_days, capacity[t])
        days_map[t] = proportional_days
        used_days += proportional_days

    # distribute remaining days
    index = 0
    while used_days < total_days:
        topic = topic_order[index % len(topic_order)]
        if days_map[topic] < capacity[topic]:
            days_map[topic] += 1
            used_days += 1
        index += 1
        if index > 10000:
            break

    return days_map


def get_difficulty_mix(day, total_days_for_topic):
    if total_days_for_topic == 1:
        return {"easy": 0.4, "medium": 0.4, "hard": 0.2}

    progress = day / total_days_for_topic

    if progress <= 0.3:
        return {"easy": 1, "medium": 0, "hard": 0}
    elif progress <= 0.6:
        return {"easy": 0.5, "medium": 0.5, "hard": 0}
    else:
        return {"easy": 0.2, "medium": 0.5, "hard": 0.3}


def pick_problems(bucket, count, cursor):
    selected = []

    for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"]:
        while len(selected) < count and cursor[lvl] < len(bucket[lvl]):
            selected.append(bucket[lvl][cursor[lvl]])
            cursor[lvl] += 1

    return selected


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# MAIN PLAN GENERATOR
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def generate_plan(all_problems, total_days):

    topic_order, topic_map = build_topic_plan(total_days)
    links_per_day = get_links_per_day(total_days)

    raw_grouped = group_by_topic_and_difficulty(all_problems)

    grouped = {}

    if topic_map:
        for parent, children in topic_map.items():
            grouped[parent] = {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []}
            for child in children:
                if child in raw_grouped:
                    for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"]:
                        grouped[parent][lvl].extend(raw_grouped[child][lvl])
    else:
        grouped = raw_grouped

    topic_days = calculate_topic_days_accurate(
        total_days, topic_order, grouped, links_per_day
    )

    plan = {}
    current_day = 1

    for topic in topic_order:

        topic_data = grouped.get(topic, {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []})
        cursor = {"easy": 0, "medium": 0, "hard": 0}

        for day_index in range(topic_days.get(topic, 1)):

            if current_day > total_days:
                break

            remaining = sum(
                len(topic_data[lvl]) - cursor[lvl]
                for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"]
            )

            # If not enough problems → revision day
            if remaining < 3:
                plan[f"Day {current_day}"] = {
                    "topic": topic,
                    "type": "revision",
                    "reference_links": get_reference_links(topic, 2),
                    "problems": [],
                    "tasks": [
                        f"Revise {topic}",
                        "Re-solve previous problems",
                        "Analyze mistakes"
                    ]
                }
                current_day += 1
                continue

            daily_limit = min(links_per_day, remaining)
            mix = get_difficulty_mix(day_index + 1, topic_days[topic])

            easy_count = max(0, int(daily_limit * mix["easy"]))
            medium_count = max(0, int(daily_limit * mix["medium"]))
            hard_count = daily_limit - easy_count - medium_count

            problems = []
            problems += pick_problems(topic_data, easy_count, cursor)
            problems += pick_problems(topic_data, medium_count, cursor)
            problems += pick_problems(topic_data, hard_count, cursor)

            plan[f"Day {current_day}"] = {
                "topic": topic,
                "type": "practice",
                "reference_links": get_reference_links(topic, 3),
                "problems": problems,
            }

            current_day += 1

    return plan


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# REVISION SHEET GENERATOR
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def create_revision_sheet(plan, total_days):

    used_days = len(plan)
    remaining_days = total_days - used_days

    if remaining_days <= 0:
        return {}

    if remaining_days <= 5:
        per_day = 20
    elif remaining_days <= 9:
        per_day = 15
    elif remaining_days <= 15:
        per_day = 12
    else:
        per_day = 10

    all_practice = [
        problem
        for value in plan.values()
        if value["type"] == "practice"
        for problem in value["problems"]
    ]

    if not all_practice:
        return {}

    random.shuffle(all_practice)

    revision_plan = {}
    pointer = 0

    for d in range(1, remaining_days + 1):

        take = min(per_day, len(all_practice))
        selected = all_practice[pointer:pointer + take]

        revision_plan[f"Revision Day {d}"] = {
            "type": "revision-practice",
            "problems": selected
        }

        pointer += take
        if pointer >= len(all_practice):
            pointer = 0
            random.shuffle(all_practice)

    return revision_plan



import requests
from django.conf import settings
from django.shortcuts import render
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.views.decorators.csrf import csrf_exempt


def csfund_view(request):
    return render(request, "csfund.html")


@csrf_exempt
def generate_csfund(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        subject = request.POST.get("subject")
        days = request.POST.get("days")

        if not subject or not days:
            return JsonResponse({"error": "Missing subject or days"})

        prompt = f"""
Create a detailed {days}-day study plan for {subject}.

IMPORTANT:
You MUST generate exactly {days} days.
Do not stop early.
Continue until Day {days} is fully completed.

Format:

## Day 1
### Topics
- Bullet points

### Practice
- Bullet points

### Resources
- Website Name - Proper URL only from geeks for geeks w3 schools and leetcode and wikipedia

Continue sequentially until Day {days}.
"""

        try:
            response = requests.post(
                "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",
                headers={
                    "Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.OPENROUTER_API_KEY}",
                    "Content-Type": "application/json"
                },
                json={
                    "model": "openai/gpt-4o-mini",
                    "messages": [
                        {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
                    ],
                    "temperature": 0.4,
                    "max_tokens":4000 
                },
                timeout=20
            )

            data = response.json()
            output = data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

            return JsonResponse({"result": output})

        except Exception as e:
            return JsonResponse({"error": str(e)})

    return JsonResponse({"error": "Invalid request"})





  



# @@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@@


import json
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST
from django.http import JsonResponse
from .models import SchedulerPlan, DayPlan

@login_required
@require_POST
def generate_plan_view(request):

    data = json.loads(request.body)
    total_days = data.get("total_days")

    if not total_days:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Days required"}, status=400)

    total_days = int(total_days)

    # 🔥 Load problems
    all_problems = load_all_problems()

    study_plan = generate_plan(all_problems, total_days)
    revision_plan = create_revision_sheet(study_plan, total_days)

    plan = SchedulerPlan.objects.create(
        user=request.user,
        title=f"DSA Plan ({total_days} days)",
        total_days=total_days
    )

    day_counter = 1

    # ✅ SAVE STUDY PLAN
    for day, value in study_plan.items():

        clean_data = {
            "topic": value.get("topic", ""),
            "type": value.get("type", ""),
            "reference_links": value.get("reference_links", []),
            "problems": value.get("problems", []),
            "tasks": value.get("tasks", [])
        }

        DayPlan.objects.create(
            plan=plan,
            day_number=day_counter,
            topic=clean_data["topic"],
            plan_type=clean_data["type"],
            data=clean_data
        )

        day_counter += 1

    # ✅ SAVE REVISION PLAN
    for day, value in revision_plan.items():

        clean_data = {
            "reference_links": value.get("reference_links", []),
            "problems": value.get("problems", []),
            "tasks": value.get("tasks", [])
        }

        DayPlan.objects.create(
            plan=plan,
            day_number=day_counter,
            topic="Revision",
            plan_type="revision",
            data=clean_data
        )

        day_counter += 1

    return JsonResponse({
        "success": True,
        "plan_id": plan.id
    })

@login_required
def get_user_plans(request):

    plans = SchedulerPlan.objects.filter(user=request.user).order_by("-created_at")

    data = []
    for p in plans:
        data.append({
            "id": p.id,
            "title": p.title,
            "created": p.created_at.strftime("%d %b %Y")
        })

    return JsonResponse({"plans": data})


@login_required
def get_plan_detail(request, plan_id):

    plan = SchedulerPlan.objects.get(id=plan_id, user=request.user)
    days = plan.days.all()

    result = []

    for d in days:

        data = d.data or {}

        result.append({
            "id": d.id,
            "day_number": d.day_number,
            "topic": d.topic,
            "type": d.plan_type,
            "reference_links": data.get("reference_links", []),
            "problems": data.get("problems", []),
            "tasks": data.get("tasks", []),
            "completed": d.completed
        })

    return JsonResponse({"plan": result})

@login_required
@require_POST
def toggle_day_complete(request, day_id):

    day = DayPlan.objects.get(id=day_id, plan__user=request.user)
    day.completed = not day.completed
    day.save()

    return JsonResponse({"completed": day.completed})


@login_required
@require_POST
def delete_plan(request, plan_id):

    plan = SchedulerPlan.objects.get(id=plan_id, user=request.user)
    plan.delete()

    return JsonResponse({"success": True})

# from .models import Scheduler, DayPlan, Task

import random
import json
from django.shortcuts import render, redirect
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.contrib.auth import authenticate, login, logout
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required, user_passes_test
from django.contrib import messages
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.views.decorators.csrf import csrf_exempt
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# AUTH VIEWS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def home(request):
    return render(request, 'home.html')


def register_view(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        username = request.POST['username']
        password = request.POST['password']

        if User.objects.filter(username=username).exists():
            messages.error(request, "Username already exists")
            return redirect('register')

        user = User.objects.create_user(username=username, password=password)
        login(request, user)
        return redirect('dashboard')

    return render(request, 'register.html')


def login_view(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        username = request.POST['username']
        password = request.POST['password']
        user = authenticate(request, username=username, password=password)

        if user is not None:
            login(request, user)
            return redirect('dashboard')
        else:
            messages.error(request, "Invalid credentials")

    return render(request, 'login.html')


def logout_view(request):
    logout(request)
    return redirect('home')


@login_required
def dashboard(request):
    return render(request, 'dashboard.html')


def admin_check(user):
    return user.is_superuser


@login_required
@user_passes_test(admin_check)
def admin_page(request):
    return render(request, 'admin_page.html')


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# SCHEDULER VIEWS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

from django.shortcuts import render

def scheduler(request):
    return render(request, "scheduler.html")


@csrf_exempt
@require_POST
def generate_plan_view(request):
    """
    POST /generate-plan/
    Body (JSON): { "total_days": <int> }
    Returns JSON with study_plan + revision_plan
    """
    try:
        body = json.loads(request.body)
        total_days = int(body.get("total_days", 0))
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError, TypeError):
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Invalid request body. Send JSON with total_days."}, status=400)

    if total_days < 1:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "total_days must be a positive integer."}, status=400)

    plan = generate_plan(allProblems, total_days)
    revision = create_revision_sheet(plan, total_days)

    return JsonResponse({
        "total_days": total_days,
        "study_plan": plan,
        "revision_plan": revision,
    })


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# DATA
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────


def load_all_problems():
    return [
  {
    "title": "Two Sum – Return indices adding to target",
    "link": "https://leetcode.com/problems/two-sum/",
    "source": "leetcode",
    "difficulty": "easy",
    "topic": "Hashing",
  },
  {
    "title": "Single Number – Every element appears twice except one",
    "link": "https://leetcode.com/problems/single-number/",
    "source": "leetcode",
    "difficulty": "easy",
    "topic": "Bit Manipulation",
  },
  {
    "title": "Remove Duplicates from Sorted Array",
    "link": "https://leetcode.com/problems/remove-duplicates-from-sorted-array/",

import random


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# REFERENCE LINKS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def get_reference_links(topic, max_links=3):
    links = REFERENCE_LINKS.get(topic, [])
    return links[:max_links] if links else []


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# PLAN ENGINE HELPERS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def build_topic_plan(total_days):

    if total_days <= 25:
        topic_order = [
            "Arrays", "Hashing", "Two Pointers", "Sliding Window",
            "Bit Manipulation", "Linked List", "Stack", "Queue",
            "Strings", "Sorting & Searching", "Trees", "Graphs",
            "Dynamic Programming", "Kadane's Algorithm",
        ]

        topic_map = {
            "Arrays": ["Array Manipulation", "Prefix Sum", "Prefix Product", "Matrix", "Math"],
            "Hashing": ["Hashing"],
            "Two Pointers": ["Two Pointers"],
            "Sliding Window": ["Sliding Window"],
            "Bit Manipulation": ["Bit Manipulation", "Boyer-Moore Voting"],
            "Linked List": ["Linked List"],
            "Stack": ["Stack"],
            "Queue": [],
            "Strings": ["String Manipulation", "String Matching"],
            "Sorting & Searching": ["Sorting", "Merge Sort", "Quickselect", "Binary Search", "Linear Search"],
            "Trees": ["Binary Tree", "Binary Search Tree", "AVL Tree", "Trie", "Segment Tree", "Fenwick Tree", "Heap"],
            "Graphs": ["Graph", "BFS", "DFS", "Dijkstra", "Shortest Path",
                       "Minimum Spanning Tree", "Topological Sort",
                       "Union Find", "Disjoint Set", "Graph Coloring"],
            "Dynamic Programming": ["Dynamic Programming", "Divide and Conquer", "Greedy", "Backtracking"],
            "Kadane's Algorithm": ["Kadane's Algorithm"],
        }
    else:
        topic_order = RAW_TOPICS
        topic_map = None

    return topic_order, topic_map


def get_links_per_day(days):
    if days <= 15:
        return 25
    if days <= 30:
        return 15
    if days <= 59:
        return 10
    if days <= 100:
        return 8
    return 5


def group_by_topic_and_difficulty(problems):
    grouped = {}
    for p in problems:
        topic = p.get("topic")
        difficulty = p.get("difficulty")

        if topic not in grouped:
            grouped[topic] = {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []}

        if difficulty in grouped[topic]:
            grouped[topic][difficulty].append(p)

    return grouped


def compute_dynamic_topic_weight(topic_order, grouped):
    weight = {}
    for t in topic_order:
        if t not in grouped:
            weight[t] = 1
        else:
            total = sum(len(grouped[t][lvl]) for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"])
            weight[t] = max(1, total)
    return weight


def calculate_topic_days_accurate(total_days, topic_order, grouped, links_per_day):

    capacity = {}

    for t in topic_order:
        if t not in grouped:
            capacity[t] = 1
        else:
            total_questions = sum(len(grouped[t][lvl]) for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"])
            capacity[t] = max(1, (total_questions + links_per_day - 1) // links_per_day)

    weight = compute_dynamic_topic_weight(topic_order, grouped)
    total_weight = sum(weight.values())

    days_map = {}
    used_days = 0

    for t in topic_order:
        proportional_days = max(1, int(weight[t] / total_weight * total_days))
        proportional_days = min(proportional_days, capacity[t])
        days_map[t] = proportional_days
        used_days += proportional_days

    # distribute remaining days
    index = 0
    while used_days < total_days:
        topic = topic_order[index % len(topic_order)]
        if days_map[topic] < capacity[topic]:
            days_map[topic] += 1
            used_days += 1
        index += 1
        if index > 10000:
            break

    return days_map


def get_difficulty_mix(day, total_days_for_topic):
    if total_days_for_topic == 1:
        return {"easy": 0.4, "medium": 0.4, "hard": 0.2}

    progress = day / total_days_for_topic

    if progress <= 0.3:
        return {"easy": 1, "medium": 0, "hard": 0}
    elif progress <= 0.6:
        return {"easy": 0.5, "medium": 0.5, "hard": 0}
    else:
        return {"easy": 0.2, "medium": 0.5, "hard": 0.3}


def pick_problems(bucket, count, cursor):
    selected = []

    for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"]:
        while len(selected) < count and cursor[lvl] < len(bucket[lvl]):
            selected.append(bucket[lvl][cursor[lvl]])
            cursor[lvl] += 1

    return selected


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# MAIN PLAN GENERATOR
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def generate_plan(all_problems, total_days):

    topic_order, topic_map = build_topic_plan(total_days)
    links_per_day = get_links_per_day(total_days)

    raw_grouped = group_by_topic_and_difficulty(all_problems)

    grouped = {}

    if topic_map:
        for parent, children in topic_map.items():
            grouped[parent] = {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []}
            for child in children:
                if child in raw_grouped:
                    for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"]:
                        grouped[parent][lvl].extend(raw_grouped[child][lvl])
    else:
        grouped = raw_grouped

    topic_days = calculate_topic_days_accurate(
        total_days, topic_order, grouped, links_per_day
    )

    plan = {}
    current_day = 1

    for topic in topic_order:

        topic_data = grouped.get(topic, {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []})
        cursor = {"easy": 0, "medium": 0, "hard": 0}

        for day_index in range(topic_days.get(topic, 1)):

            if current_day > total_days:
                break

            remaining = sum(
                len(topic_data[lvl]) - cursor[lvl]
                for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"]
            )

            # If not enough problems → revision day
            if remaining < 3:
                plan[f"Day {current_day}"] = {
                    "topic": topic,
                    "type": "revision",
                    "reference_links": get_reference_links(topic, 2),
                    "problems": [],
                    "tasks": [
                        f"Revise {topic}",
                        "Re-solve previous problems",
                        "Analyze mistakes"
                    ]
                }
                current_day += 1
                continue

            daily_limit = min(links_per_day, remaining)
            mix = get_difficulty_mix(day_index + 1, topic_days[topic])

            easy_count = max(0, int(daily_limit * mix["easy"]))
            medium_count = max(0, int(daily_limit * mix["medium"]))
            hard_count = daily_limit - easy_count - medium_count

            problems = []
            problems += pick_problems(topic_data, easy_count, cursor)
            problems += pick_problems(topic_data, medium_count, cursor)
            problems += pick_problems(topic_data, hard_count, cursor)

            plan[f"Day {current_day}"] = {
                "topic": topic,
                "type": "practice",
                "reference_links": get_reference_links(topic, 3),
                "problems": problems,
            }

            current_day += 1

    return plan


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# REVISION SHEET GENERATOR
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def create_revision_sheet(plan, total_days):

    used_days = len(plan)
    remaining_days = total_days - used_days

    if remaining_days <= 0:
        return {}

    if remaining_days <= 5:
        per_day = 20
    elif remaining_days <= 9:
        per_day = 15
    elif remaining_days <= 15:
        per_day = 12
    else:
        per_day = 10

    all_practice = [
        problem
        for value in plan.values()
        if value["type"] == "practice"
        for problem in value["problems"]
    ]

    if not all_practice:
        return {}

    random.shuffle(all_practice)

    revision_plan = {}
    pointer = 0

    for d in range(1, remaining_days + 1):

        take = min(per_day, len(all_practice))
        selected = all_practice[pointer:pointer + take]

        revision_plan[f"Revision Day {d}"] = {
            "type": "revision-practice",
            "problems": selected
        }

        pointer += take
        if pointer >= len(all_practice):
            pointer = 0
            random.shuffle(all_practice)

    return revision_plan

from django.shortcuts import render, get_object_or_404
from django.http import JsonResponse, StreamingHttpResponse
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import transaction

from .models import CSFundPlan, CSDayPlan

import json
import requests
import re

@login_required
def csfund_view(request):
    return render(request, "csfund.html")


@login_required
@require_POST
def generate_csfund(request):
    subject = request.POST.get("subject", "").strip()
    days = request.POST.get("days", "").strip()

    if not subject or not days:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Missing subject or days"}, status=400)

    try:
        days = int(days)
        if days <= 0 or days > 60:
            return JsonResponse({"error": "Days must be between 1 and 60"}, status=400)
    except ValueError:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Invalid days value"}, status=400)

    prompt = f"""
You are an expert tutor, curriculum designer, and interview coach. Create a comprehensive {days}-day study plan for the subject "{subject}", AND provide the 10 most important interview questions and answers related to this subject. 

Requirements for the study plan:
1. Exactly {days} days, each labeled "Day 1", "Day 2", etc.
2. Each day must have:
   - ### Topics
     - List of key topics (concise, clear, actionable)
   - ### Practice
     - Exercises or small tasks to reinforce the topics
   - ### Resources
     - Provide real reference links (articles, tutorials, documentation, or videos)
     - Include the source name and a working URL
3. Ensure all resources are accurate, up-to-date, and publicly accessible.
4. Keep the format strictly as shown; no extra text outside the format.
5. Include examples, code snippets, or diagrams if relevant.

Requirements for interview questions:
1. After the study plan, list 10 top interview questions for {subject}.
2. Each question must have a clear, precise, and correct answer.
3. Format strictly as:
   - **Q1:** Question text
     **A1:** Answer text
"""

    def stream():
        try:
            response = requests.post(
                "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",
                headers={
                    "Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.OPENROUTER_API_KEY}",
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                },
                json={
                    "model": "openai/gpt-4o-mini",
                    "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                    "temperature": 0.4,
                    "max_tokens": 4000,
                },
                timeout=120,
            )

            response.raise_for_status()
            output = response.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

        except Exception as e:
            yield json.dumps({"error": str(e)}) + "\n"
            return

        with transaction.atomic():
            plan = CSFundPlan.objects.create(
                user=request.user,
                subject=subject,
                total_days=days
            )

            # Correct parsing: keep "## Day N" with content
            day_blocks = re.findall(r"(## Day \d+.*?)(?=## Day \d+|# Top 10 Interview Questions|$)", output, flags=re.S)
            day_number = 1

            for block in day_blocks:
                block = block.strip()
                if not block:
                    continue

                day = CSDayPlan.objects.create(
                    plan=plan,
                    day_number=day_number,
                    topic=f"{subject} - Day {day_number}",
                    data={"content": block},
                )

                yield json.dumps({
                    "id": day.id,
                    "plan_id": plan.id,
                    "day_number": day_number,
                    "content": block,
                    "completed": False,
                }) + "\n"

                day_number += 1

        yield json.dumps({"done": True, "plan_id": plan.id}) + "\n"

    return StreamingHttpResponse(stream(), content_type="application/json", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"})

@login_required
def get_cs_plans(request):
    plans = CSFundPlan.objects.filter(
        user=request.user
    ).order_by("-created_at")

    data = [
        {
            "id": p.id,
            "subject": p.subject,
            "created": p.created_at.strftime("%d %b %Y"),
        }
        for p in plans
    ]

    return JsonResponse({"plans": data})


@login_required
def get_cs_plan_detail(request, plan_id):

    plan = get_object_or_404(
        CSFundPlan,
        id=plan_id,
        user=request.user
    )

    days = plan.days.all().order_by("day_number")

    result = [
        {
            "id": d.id,
            "day_number": d.day_number,
            "topic": d.topic,
            "content": d.data.get("content"),
            "completed": d.completed,
        }
        for d in days
    ]

    return JsonResponse({"plan": result})


@login_required
@require_POST
def toggle_cs_day(request, day_id):

    day = get_object_or_404(
        CSDayPlan,
        id=day_id,
        plan__user=request.user
    )

    day.completed = not day.completed
    day.save()

    return JsonResponse({"completed": day.completed})


@login_required
@require_POST
def delete_cs_plan(request, plan_id):
    plan = CSFundPlan.objects.get(id=plan_id, user=request.user)
    plan.delete()
    return JsonResponse({"success": True})

import json
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST
from django.http import JsonResponse
from .models import SchedulerPlan, DayPlan

@login_required
@require_POST
def generate_plan_view(request):

    data = json.loads(request.body)

    title = data.get("title", "").strip()
    total_days = data.get("total_days")

    if not title:
        title = f"DSA Plan ({total_days} days)"

    if not total_days:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Days required"}, status=400)

    total_days = int(total_days)

    # 🔥 Load problems
    all_problems = load_all_problems()

    study_plan = generate_plan(all_problems, total_days)
    revision_plan = create_revision_sheet(study_plan, total_days)

    plan = SchedulerPlan.objects.create(
    user=request.user,
    title=title,
    total_days=total_days
    )

    day_counter = 1

    # ✅ SAVE STUDY PLAN
    for day, value in study_plan.items():

        clean_data = {
            "topic": value.get("topic", ""),
            "type": value.get("type", ""),
            "reference_links": value.get("reference_links", []),
            "problems": value.get("problems", []),
            "tasks": value.get("tasks", [])
        }

        DayPlan.objects.create(
            plan=plan,
            day_number=day_counter,
            topic=clean_data["topic"],
            plan_type=clean_data["type"],
            data=clean_data
        )

        day_counter += 1

    # ✅ SAVE REVISION PLAN
    for day, value in revision_plan.items():

        clean_data = {
            "reference_links": value.get("reference_links", []),
            "problems": value.get("problems", []),
            "tasks": value.get("tasks", [])
        }

        DayPlan.objects.create(
            plan=plan,
            day_number=day_counter,
            topic="Revision",
            plan_type="revision",
            data=clean_data
        )

        day_counter += 1

    return JsonResponse({
        "success": True,
        "plan_id": plan.id
    })

@login_required
def get_user_plans(request):

    plans = SchedulerPlan.objects.filter(user=request.user).order_by("-created_at")

    data = []
    for p in plans:
        data.append({
            "id": p.id,
            "title": p.title,
            "days": p.total_days
        })

    return JsonResponse({"plans": data})


@login_required
def get_plan_detail(request, plan_id):

    plan = SchedulerPlan.objects.get(id=plan_id, user=request.user)
    days = plan.days.all()

    result = []

    for d in days:

        data = d.data or {}

        result.append({
            "id": d.id,
            "day_number": d.day_number,
            "topic": d.topic,
            "type": d.plan_type,
            "reference_links": data.get("reference_links", []),
            "problems": data.get("problems", []),
            "tasks": data.get("tasks", []),
            "completed": d.completed
        })

    return JsonResponse({"plan": result})

@login_required
@require_POST

    day = DayPlan.objects.get(id=day_id, plan__user=request.user)
    day.completed = not day.completed
    day.save()

    return JsonResponse({"completed": day.completed})


@login_required
@require_POST
def delete_plan(request, plan_id):

    plan = SchedulerPlan.objects.get(id=plan_id, user=request.user)
    plan.delete()

    return JsonResponse({"success": True})

import json
import requests
from django.conf import settings
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.shortcuts import render
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required


@login_required
def chatbot_view(request):
    return render(request, "chatbot.html")


@login_required
def chat_api(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        try:
            data = json.loads(request.body)
            message = data.get("message")

            headers = {
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.OPENROUTER_API_KEY}",
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                "HTTP-Referer": "http://localhost:8000",
                "X-Title": "Coding Chatbot"
            }

            body = {
                "model": "openai/gpt-3.5-turbo",
                "messages": [
                    {
                        "role": "system",
                        "content": "You are a coding assistant. Reply with correct code and explanation."
                    },
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": message
                    }
                ],
                "temperature": 0.2
            }

            response = requests.post(
                "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",
                headers=headers,
                json=body,
                timeout=60
            )

            if response.status_code != 200:
                return JsonResponse({"error": response.text}, status=500)

            result = response.json()
            reply = result["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

            return JsonResponse({"reply": reply})

        except Exception as e:
            return JsonResponse({"error": str(e)}, status=500)

    return JsonResponse({"error": "Invalid request"}, status=400)

import time
from django.shortcuts import render

def read_sheet_cached(user, worksheet):
    return worksheet.get_all_values()


@login_required
def track(request):
    return render(request, "performance.html")


@login_required
@require_POST
def init_tracker(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)
    days = int(data.get("days", 1))

    worksheet = get_or_create_user_tab(request.user)
    worksheet.clear()

    headers = ["Task"] + [f"Day {i+1}" for i in range(days)]
    worksheet.append_row(headers)

    return JsonResponse({"status": "ok"})


@login_required
def get_data(request):
    worksheet = get_or_create_user_tab(request.user)
    rows = read_sheet_cached(request.user, worksheet)

    if not rows:
        return JsonResponse({"days": 0, "tasks": []})

    headers = rows[0]
    tasks = []

    for row in rows[1:]:
        progress = [int(x) if x else 0 for x in row[1:]]
        tasks.append({"name": row[0], "progress": progress})

    return JsonResponse({
        "days": len(headers) - 1,
        "tasks": tasks
    })


@login_required
@require_POST
def add_task(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)
    name = data.get("name")

    worksheet = get_or_create_user_tab(request.user)
    days = len(worksheet.row_values(1)) - 1

    worksheet.append_row([name] + [0] * days)

    return JsonResponse({"success": True})


@login_required
@require_POST
def toggle(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)

    row = int(data["task_index"]) + 2
    col = int(data["day_index"]) + 2
    value = int(data["value"])

    worksheet = get_or_create_user_tab(request.user)
    worksheet.update_cell(row, col, value)

    return JsonResponse({"success": True})


@login_required
@require_POST
def add_day(request):
    worksheet = get_or_create_user_tab(request.user)
    headers = worksheet.row_values(1)
    new_col = len(headers) + 1

    worksheet.update_cell(1, new_col, f"Day {new_col-1}")

    rows = worksheet.get_all_values()
    if len(rows) > 1:
        cells = worksheet.range(2, new_col, len(rows), new_col)
        for cell in cells:
            cell.value = 0
        worksheet.update_cells(cells)

    return JsonResponse({"success": True})


@login_required
@require_POST
def remove_day(request):
    worksheet = get_or_create_user_tab(request.user)
    headers = worksheet.row_values(1)

    if len(headers) <= 2:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Minimum 1 day required"})

    worksheet.delete_columns(len(headers))

    return JsonResponse({"success": True})


@login_required
@require_POST
def delete_task(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)
    row = int(data["task_index"]) + 2

    worksheet = get_or_create_user_tab(request.user)
    worksheet.delete_rows(row)

    return JsonResponse({"success": True})


@login_required
@require_POST
def reset_tracker(request):
    worksheet = get_or_create_user_tab(request.user)
    worksheet.clear()

    return JsonResponse({"status": "reset"})


@login_required
def chart_data(request):
    worksheet = get_or_create_user_tab(request.user)
    rows = read_sheet_cached(request.user, worksheet)

    if len(rows) <= 1:
        return JsonResponse({"bar": [], "line": []})

    header = rows[0][1:]
    data = rows[1:]

    bar = []
    for row in data:
        completed = row[1:].count("1")
        total = len(row[1:])
        percent = round((completed / total) * 100, 2) if total else 0
        bar.append({"task": row[0], "percent": percent})

    line = []
    for i in range(len(header)):
        done = sum(1 for row in data if row[i + 1] == "1")
        line.append({"day": header[i], "value": done})

    return JsonResponse({"bar": bar, "line": line})


from django.db.models import Avg, Sum
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.shortcuts import render
from .models import SchedulerPlan, DayPlan, CSFundPlan, CSDayPlan

@login_required
def scheduler_progress(request):
    return render(request, "scheduler_progress.html")


@login_required
def scheduler_progress_api(request):
    user = request.user

    all_progress = []

    # ─── DSA / Scheduler Plans ───
    scheduler_plans = SchedulerPlan.objects.filter(user=user)
    for plan in scheduler_plans:
        days = plan.days.all()
        total = days.count()
        completed = days.filter(completed=True).count()
        time_spent = days.aggregate(Sum("time_spent"))["time_spent__sum"] or 0
        avg_score = days.aggregate(Avg("score"))["score__avg"] or 0
        percent = round((completed / total) * 100, 2) if total else 0

        all_progress.append({
            "name": plan.title,
            "total_days": total,
            "completed": completed,
            "percent": percent,
            "time_spent": time_spent,
            "avg_score": round(avg_score, 2)
        })

    # ─── CS Fundamentals Plans ───
    cs_plans = CSFundPlan.objects.filter(user=user)
    for plan in cs_plans:
        days = plan.days.all()
        total = days.count()
        completed = days.filter(completed=True).count()
        percent = round((completed / total) * 100, 2) if total else 0

        all_progress.append({
            "name": plan.subject,
            "total_days": total,
            "completed": completed,
            "percent": percent,
            "time_spent": 0,  # optional if not tracked
            "avg_score": 0    # optional if not tracked
        })

    return JsonResponse({"plans": all_progress})





# Utility: group problems by topic and difficulty
def group_by_topic_and_difficulty(problems):
    grouped = {}
    for p in problems:
        topic = p["topic"]
        diff = p["difficulty"].lower()
        if topic not in grouped:
            grouped[topic] = {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []}
        if diff in grouped[topic]:
            grouped[topic][diff].append(p)
    return grouped



def get_reference_links(topic, count=3):
    return [f"https://example.com/{topic.replace(' ','-')}-{i+1}" for i in range(count)]

@login_required
@require_POST
def generate_specific_plan(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)
    topics = data.get("topics", [])

    if not topics:
        return JsonResponse({"success": False})

    all_problems = load_all_problems()
    grouped = group_by_topic_and_difficulty(all_problems)

    plan = {}
    day = 1

    for topic in topics:
        topic_data = grouped.get(topic)
        if not topic_data:
            continue

        # Merge all problems for this topic (easy + medium + hard)
        all_topic_problems = topic_data.get("easy", []) + topic_data.get("medium", []) + topic_data.get("hard", [])

        plan[f"Day {day}"] = {
            "topic": topic,
            "type": "practice",
            "reference_links": get_reference_links(topic, 3),
            "problems": all_topic_problems
        }
        day += 1

    request.session["specific_plan"] = plan
    request.session.modified = True

    return JsonResponse({"success": True, "plan": plan})

@login_required
def get_specific_plan(request):
    plan = request.session.get("specific_plan")
    if not plan:
        return JsonResponse({"success": False})
    return JsonResponse({"success": True, "plan": plan})

@login_required
def specificschedule_view(request):
    return render(request, "specificschedule.html")





@login_required
def mock_test(request):
    return render(request, "mock_test.html")

import random
import json
from django.shortcuts import render, redirect
from django.contrib.auth.models import User
from django.contrib.auth import authenticate, login, logout
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required, user_passes_test

from django.views.decorators.csrf import csrf_exempt
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# AUTH VIEWS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def home(request):
    return render(request, 'home.html')


def register_view(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        username = request.POST['username']
        password = request.POST['password']

        if User.objects.filter(username=username).exists():
            messages.error(request, "Username already exists")
            return redirect('register')

        user = User.objects.create_user(username=username, password=password)
        login(request, user)
        return redirect('dashboard')

    return render(request, 'register.html')


def login_view(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        username = request.POST['username']
        password = request.POST['password']
        user = authenticate(request, username=username, password=password)

        if user is not None:
            login(request, user)
            return redirect('dashboard')
        else:
            messages.error(request, "Invalid credentials")

    return render(request, 'login.html')


def logout_view(request):
    logout(request)
    return redirect('home')


@login_required
def dashboard(request):
    return render(request, 'dashboard.html')


def admin_check(user):
    return user.is_superuser


@login_required
@user_passes_test(admin_check)
def admin_page(request):
    return render(request, 'admin_page.html')

from django.shortcuts import render

def scheduler(request):
    return render(request, "scheduler.html")


@csrf_exempt
@require_POST
def generate_plan_view(request):
    """
    POST /generate-plan/
    Body (JSON): { "total_days": <int> }
    Returns JSON with study_plan + revision_plan
    """
    try:
        body = json.loads(request.body)
        total_days = int(body.get("total_days", 0))
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError, TypeError):
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Invalid request body. Send JSON with total_days."}, status=400)

    if total_days < 1:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "total_days must be a positive integer."}, status=400)

    plan = generate_plan(allProblems, total_days)
    revision = create_revision_sheet(plan, total_days)

    return JsonResponse({
        "total_days": total_days,
        "study_plan": plan,
        "revision_plan": revision,
    })

import random


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# REFERENCE LINKS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def get_reference_links(topic, max_links=3):
    links = REFERENCE_LINKS.get(topic, [])
    return links[:max_links] if links else []


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# PLAN ENGINE HELPERS
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def build_topic_plan(total_days):

    if total_days <= 25:
        topic_order = [
            "Arrays", "Hashing", "Two Pointers", "Sliding Window",
            "Bit Manipulation", "Linked List", "Stack", "Queue",
            "Strings", "Sorting & Searching", "Trees", "Graphs",
            "Dynamic Programming", "Kadane's Algorithm",
        ]

        topic_map = {
            "Arrays": ["Array Manipulation", "Prefix Sum", "Prefix Product", "Matrix", "Math"],
            "Hashing": ["Hashing"],
            "Two Pointers": ["Two Pointers"],
            "Sliding Window": ["Sliding Window"],
            "Bit Manipulation": ["Bit Manipulation", "Boyer-Moore Voting"],
            "Linked List": ["Linked List"],
            "Stack": ["Stack"],
            "Queue": [],
            "Strings": ["String Manipulation", "String Matching"],
            "Sorting & Searching": ["Sorting", "Merge Sort", "Quickselect", "Binary Search", "Linear Search"],
            "Trees": ["Binary Tree", "Binary Search Tree", "AVL Tree", "Trie", "Segment Tree", "Fenwick Tree", "Heap"],
            "Graphs": ["Graph", "BFS", "DFS", "Dijkstra", "Shortest Path",
                       "Minimum Spanning Tree", "Topological Sort",
                       "Union Find", "Disjoint Set", "Graph Coloring"],
            "Dynamic Programming": ["Dynamic Programming", "Divide and Conquer", "Greedy", "Backtracking"],
            "Kadane's Algorithm": ["Kadane's Algorithm"],
        }
    else:
        topic_order = RAW_TOPICS
        topic_map = None

    return topic_order, topic_map


def get_links_per_day(days):
    if days <= 15:
        return 25
    if days <= 30:
        return 15
    if days <= 59:
        return 10
    if days <= 100:
        return 8
    return 5


def group_by_topic_and_difficulty(problems):
    grouped = {}
    for p in problems:
        topic = p.get("topic")
        difficulty = p.get("difficulty")

        if topic not in grouped:
            grouped[topic] = {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []}

        if difficulty in grouped[topic]:
            grouped[topic][difficulty].append(p)

    return grouped


def compute_dynamic_topic_weight(topic_order, grouped):
    weight = {}
    for t in topic_order:
        if t not in grouped:
            weight[t] = 1
        else:
            total = sum(len(grouped[t][lvl]) for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"])
            weight[t] = max(1, total)
    return weight


def calculate_topic_days_accurate(total_days, topic_order, grouped, links_per_day):

    capacity = {}

    for t in topic_order:
        if t not in grouped:
            capacity[t] = 1
        else:
            total_questions = sum(len(grouped[t][lvl]) for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"])
            capacity[t] = max(1, (total_questions + links_per_day - 1) // links_per_day)

    weight = compute_dynamic_topic_weight(topic_order, grouped)
    total_weight = sum(weight.values())

    days_map = {}
    used_days = 0

    for t in topic_order:
        proportional_days = max(1, int(weight[t] / total_weight * total_days))
        proportional_days = min(proportional_days, capacity[t])
        days_map[t] = proportional_days
        used_days += proportional_days

    # distribute remaining days
    index = 0
    while used_days < total_days:
        topic = topic_order[index % len(topic_order)]
        if days_map[topic] < capacity[topic]:
            days_map[topic] += 1
            used_days += 1
        index += 1
        if index > 10000:
            break

    return days_map


def get_difficulty_mix(day, total_days_for_topic):
    if total_days_for_topic == 1:
        return {"easy": 0.4, "medium": 0.4, "hard": 0.2}

    progress = day / total_days_for_topic

    if progress <= 0.3:
        return {"easy": 1, "medium": 0, "hard": 0}
    elif progress <= 0.6:
        return {"easy": 0.5, "medium": 0.5, "hard": 0}
    else:
        return {"easy": 0.2, "medium": 0.5, "hard": 0.3}


def pick_problems(bucket, count, cursor):
    selected = []

    for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"]:
        while len(selected) < count and cursor[lvl] < len(bucket[lvl]):
            selected.append(bucket[lvl][cursor[lvl]])
            cursor[lvl] += 1

    return selected


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# MAIN PLAN GENERATOR
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def generate_plan(all_problems, total_days):

    topic_order, topic_map = build_topic_plan(total_days)
    links_per_day = get_links_per_day(total_days)

    raw_grouped = group_by_topic_and_difficulty(all_problems)

    grouped = {}

    if topic_map:
        for parent, children in topic_map.items():
            grouped[parent] = {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []}
            for child in children:
                if child in raw_grouped:
                    for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"]:
                        grouped[parent][lvl].extend(raw_grouped[child][lvl])
    else:
        grouped = raw_grouped

    topic_days = calculate_topic_days_accurate(
        total_days, topic_order, grouped, links_per_day
    )

    plan = {}
    current_day = 1

    for topic in topic_order:

        topic_data = grouped.get(topic, {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []})
        cursor = {"easy": 0, "medium": 0, "hard": 0}

        for day_index in range(topic_days.get(topic, 1)):

            if current_day > total_days:
                break

            remaining = sum(
                len(topic_data[lvl]) - cursor[lvl]
                for lvl in ["easy", "medium", "hard"]
            )

            # If not enough problems → revision day
            if remaining < 3:
                plan[f"Day {current_day}"] = {
                    "topic": topic,
                    "type": "revision",
                    "reference_links": get_reference_links(topic, 2),
                    "problems": [],
                    "tasks": [
                        f"Revise {topic}",
                        "Re-solve previous problems",
                        "Analyze mistakes"
                    ]
                }
                current_day += 1
                continue

            daily_limit = min(links_per_day, remaining)
            mix = get_difficulty_mix(day_index + 1, topic_days[topic])

            easy_count = max(0, int(daily_limit * mix["easy"]))
            medium_count = max(0, int(daily_limit * mix["medium"]))
            hard_count = daily_limit - easy_count - medium_count

            problems = []
            problems += pick_problems(topic_data, easy_count, cursor)
            problems += pick_problems(topic_data, medium_count, cursor)
            problems += pick_problems(topic_data, hard_count, cursor)

            plan[f"Day {current_day}"] = {
                "topic": topic,
                "type": "practice",
                "reference_links": get_reference_links(topic, 3),
                "problems": problems,
            }

            current_day += 1

    return plan


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# REVISION SHEET GENERATOR
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def create_revision_sheet(plan, total_days):

    used_days = len(plan)
    remaining_days = total_days - used_days

    if remaining_days <= 0:
        return {}

    if remaining_days <= 5:
        per_day = 20
    elif remaining_days <= 9:
        per_day = 15
    elif remaining_days <= 15:
        per_day = 12
    else:
        per_day = 10

    all_practice = [
        problem
        for value in plan.values()
        if value["type"] == "practice"
        for problem in value["problems"]
    ]

    if not all_practice:
        return {}

    random.shuffle(all_practice)

    revision_plan = {}
    pointer = 0

    for d in range(1, remaining_days + 1):

        take = min(per_day, len(all_practice))
        selected = all_practice[pointer:pointer + take]

        revision_plan[f"Revision Day {d}"] = {
            "type": "revision-practice",
            "problems": selected
        }

        pointer += take
        if pointer >= len(all_practice):
            pointer = 0
            random.shuffle(all_practice)

    return revision_plan

from django.shortcuts import render, get_object_or_404
from django.http import JsonResponse, StreamingHttpResponse
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import transaction

from .models import CSFundPlan, CSDayPlan

import json
import requests
import re

@login_required
def csfund_view(request):
    return render(request, "csfund.html")















@login_required
@require_POST
def generate_csfund(request):
    subject = request.POST.get("subject", "").strip()
    days = request.POST.get("days", "").strip()

    if not subject or not days:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Missing subject or days"}, status=400)

    try:
        days = int(days)
        if days <= 0 or days > 60:
            return JsonResponse({"error": "Days must be between 1 and 60"}, status=400)
    except ValueError:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Invalid days value"}, status=400)

    prompt = f"""
You are an expert tutor, curriculum designer, and interview coach. Create a comprehensive {days}-day study plan for the subject "{subject}", AND provide the 10 most important interview questions and answers related to this subject. 

Requirements for the study plan:
1. Exactly {days} days, each labeled "Day 1", "Day 2", etc.
2. Each day must have:
   - ### Topics
     - List of key topics (concise, clear, actionable)
   - ### Practice
     - Exercises or small tasks to reinforce the topics
   - ### Resources
     - Provide real reference links (articles, tutorials, documentation, or videos)
     - Include the source name and a working URL
3. Ensure all resources are accurate, up-to-date, and publicly accessible.
4. Keep the format strictly as shown; no extra text outside the format.
5. Include examples, code snippets, or diagrams if relevant.

Requirements for interview questions:
1. After the study plan, list 10 top interview questions for {subject}.
2. Each question must have a clear, precise, and correct answer.
3. Format strictly as:
   - **Q1:** Question text
     **A1:** Answer text
"""

    def stream():
        try:
            response = requests.post(
                "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",
                headers={
                    "Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.OPENROUTER_API_KEY}",
                    "Content-Type": "application/json",
                },
                json={
                    "model": "openai/gpt-4o-mini",
                    "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                    "temperature": 0.4,
                    "max_tokens": 4000,
                },
                timeout=120,
            )

            response.raise_for_status()
            output = response.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

        except Exception as e:
            yield json.dumps({"error": str(e)}) + "\n"
            return

        with transaction.atomic():
            plan = CSFundPlan.objects.create(
                user=request.user,
                subject=subject,
                total_days=days
            )

            # Correct parsing: keep "## Day N" with content
            day_blocks = re.findall(r"(## Day \d+.*?)(?=## Day \d+|# Top 10 Interview Questions|$)", output, flags=re.S)
            day_number = 1

            for block in day_blocks:
                block = block.strip()
                if not block:
                    continue

                day = CSDayPlan.objects.create(
                    plan=plan,
                    day_number=day_number,
                    topic=f"{subject} - Day {day_number}",
                    data={"content": block},
                )

                yield json.dumps({
                    "id": day.id,
                    "plan_id": plan.id,
                    "day_number": day_number,
                    "content": block,
                    "completed": False,
                }) + "\n"

                day_number += 1

        yield json.dumps({"done": True, "plan_id": plan.id}) + "\n"

    return StreamingHttpResponse(stream(), content_type="application/json", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"})













@login_required
def get_cs_plans(request):
    plans = CSFundPlan.objects.filter(
        user=request.user
    ).order_by("-created_at")

    data = [
        {
            "id": p.id,
            "subject": p.subject,
            "created": p.created_at.strftime("%d %b %Y"),
        }
        for p in plans
    ]

    return JsonResponse({"plans": data})


@login_required
def get_cs_plan_detail(request, plan_id):

    plan = get_object_or_404(
        CSFundPlan,
        id=plan_id,
        user=request.user
    )

    days = plan.days.all().order_by("day_number")

    result = [
        {
            "id": d.id,
            "day_number": d.day_number,
            "topic": d.topic,
            "content": d.data.get("content"),
            "completed": d.completed,
        }
        for d in days
    ]

    return JsonResponse({"plan": result})


@login_required
@require_POST
def toggle_cs_day(request, day_id):

    day = get_object_or_404(
        CSDayPlan,
        id=day_id,
        plan__user=request.user
    )

    day.completed = not day.completed
    day.save()

    return JsonResponse({"completed": day.completed})


@login_required
@require_POST
def delete_cs_plan(request, plan_id):
    plan = CSFundPlan.objects.get(id=plan_id, user=request.user)
    plan.delete()
    return JsonResponse({"success": True})

import json
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST
from django.http import JsonResponse
from .models import SchedulerPlan, DayPlan

@login_required
@require_POST
def generate_plan_view(request):

    data = json.loads(request.body)

    title = data.get("title", "").strip()
    total_days = data.get("total_days")

    if not title:
        title = f"DSA Plan ({total_days} days)"

    if not total_days:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Days required"}, status=400)

    total_days = int(total_days)

    # 🔥 Load problems
    all_problems = load_all_problems()

    study_plan = generate_plan(all_problems, total_days)
    revision_plan = create_revision_sheet(study_plan, total_days)

    plan = SchedulerPlan.objects.create(
    user=request.user,
    title=title,
    total_days=total_days
    )

    day_counter = 1

    # ✅ SAVE STUDY PLAN
    for day, value in study_plan.items():

        clean_data = {
            "topic": value.get("topic", ""),
            "type": value.get("type", ""),
            "reference_links": value.get("reference_links", []),
            "problems": value.get("problems", []),
            "tasks": value.get("tasks", [])
        }

        DayPlan.objects.create(
            plan=plan,
            day_number=day_counter,
            topic=clean_data["topic"],
            plan_type=clean_data["type"],
            data=clean_data
        )

        day_counter += 1

    # ✅ SAVE REVISION PLAN
    for day, value in revision_plan.items():

        clean_data = {
            "reference_links": value.get("reference_links", []),
            "problems": value.get("problems", []),
            "tasks": value.get("tasks", [])
        }

        DayPlan.objects.create(
            plan=plan,
            day_number=day_counter,
            topic="Revision",
            plan_type="revision",
            data=clean_data
        )

        day_counter += 1

    return JsonResponse({
        "success": True,
        "plan_id": plan.id
    })

@login_required

@login_required
def get_plan_detail(request, plan_id):

    plan = SchedulerPlan.objects.get(id=plan_id, user=request.user)
    days = plan.days.all()

    result = []

    for d in days:

        data = d.data or {}

        result.append({
            "id": d.id,
            "day_number": d.day_number,
            "topic": d.topic,
            "type": d.plan_type,
            "reference_links": data.get("reference_links", []),
            "problems": data.get("problems", []),
            "tasks": data.get("tasks", []),
            "completed": d.completed
        })

    return JsonResponse({"plan": result})

import json
import requests
from django.conf import settings
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.shortcuts import render
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required


@login_required
def chatbot_view(request):
    return render(request, "chatbot.html")


@login_required
def chat_api(request):
    if request.method == "POST":
        try:
            data = json.loads(request.body)
            message = data.get("message")

            headers = {
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.OPENROUTER_API_KEY}",
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                "HTTP-Referer": "http://localhost:8000",
                "X-Title": "Coding Chatbot"
            }

            body = {
                "model": "openai/gpt-3.5-turbo",
                "messages": [
                    {
                        "role": "system",
                        "content": "You are a coding assistant. Reply with correct code and explanation."
                    },
                    {
                        "role": "user",
                        "content": message
                    }
                ],
                "temperature": 0.2
            }

            response = requests.post(
                "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions",
                headers=headers,
                json=body,
                timeout=60
            )

            if response.status_code != 200:
                return JsonResponse({"error": response.text}, status=500)

            result = response.json()
            reply = result["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

            return JsonResponse({"reply": reply})

        except Exception as e:
            return JsonResponse({"error": str(e)}, status=500)

    return JsonResponse({"error": "Invalid request"}, status=400)

from django.db.models import Avg, Sum
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.shortcuts import render
from .models import SchedulerPlan, DayPlan, CSFundPlan, CSDayPlan

@login_required
def scheduler_progress(request):
    return render(request, "scheduler_progress.html")


@login_required
def scheduler_progress_api(request):
    user = request.user

    all_progress = []

    # ─── DSA / Scheduler Plans ───
    scheduler_plans = SchedulerPlan.objects.filter(user=user)
    for plan in scheduler_plans:
        days = plan.days.all()
        total = days.count()
        completed = days.filter(completed=True).count()
        time_spent = days.aggregate(Sum("time_spent"))["time_spent__sum"] or 0
        avg_score = days.aggregate(Avg("score"))["score__avg"] or 0
        percent = round((completed / total) * 100, 2) if total else 0

        all_progress.append({
            "name": plan.title,
            "total_days": total,
            "completed": completed,
            "percent": percent,
            "time_spent": time_spent,
            "avg_score": round(avg_score, 2)
        })

    # ─── CS Fundamentals Plans ───
    cs_plans = CSFundPlan.objects.filter(user=user)
    for plan in cs_plans:
        days = plan.days.all()
        total = days.count()
        completed = days.filter(completed=True).count()
        percent = round((completed / total) * 100, 2) if total else 0

        all_progress.append({
            "name": plan.subject,
            "total_days": total,
            "completed": completed,
            "percent": percent,
            "time_spent": 0,  # optional if not tracked
            "avg_score": 0    # optional if not tracked
        })

    return JsonResponse({"plans": all_progress})





# Utility: group problems by topic and difficulty
def group_by_topic_and_difficulty(problems):
    grouped = {}
    for p in problems:
        topic = p["topic"]
        diff = p["difficulty"].lower()
        if topic not in grouped:
            grouped[topic] = {"easy": [], "medium": [], "hard": []}
        if diff in grouped[topic]:
            grouped[topic][diff].append(p)
    return grouped



def get_reference_links(topic, count=3):
    return [f"https://example.com/{topic.replace(' ','-')}-{i+1}" for i in range(count)]

@login_required
@require_POST
def generate_specific_plan(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)
    topics = data.get("topics", [])

    if not topics:
        return JsonResponse({"success": False})

    all_problems = load_all_problems()
    grouped = group_by_topic_and_difficulty(all_problems)

    plan = {}
    day = 1

    for topic in topics:
        topic_data = grouped.get(topic)
        if not topic_data:
            continue

        # Merge all problems for this topic (easy + medium + hard)
        all_topic_problems = topic_data.get("easy", []) + topic_data.get("medium", []) + topic_data.get("hard", [])

        plan[f"Day {day}"] = {
            "topic": topic,
            "type": "practice",
            "reference_links": get_reference_links(topic, 3),
            "problems": all_topic_problems
        }
        day += 1

    request.session["specific_plan"] = plan
    request.session.modified = True

    return JsonResponse({"success": True, "plan": plan})

@login_required
def get_specific_plan(request):
    plan = request.session.get("specific_plan")
    if not plan:
        return JsonResponse({"success": False})
    return JsonResponse({"success": True, "plan": plan})

from django.shortcuts import render

# models.py
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST
from django.http import JsonResponse
import json
from .models import Task, TrackerSetting

@login_required
@require_POST
def init_tracker(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)
    days = int(data.get("days", 1))

    # Delete old tasks
    Task.objects.filter(user=request.user).delete()

    # Save or update tracker setting
    setting, created = TrackerSetting.objects.get_or_create(user=request.user)
    setting.days = days
    setting.save()

    return JsonResponse({"status": "ok", "days": days})

@login_required
@require_POST
def add_task(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)
    name = data.get("name")
    tasks = Task.objects.filter(user=request.user)
    max_day = Progress.objects.filter(task__in=tasks).aggregate(models.Max("day"))["day__max"] or 1
    task = Task.objects.create(user=request.user, name=name)
    for day in range(1, max_day + 1):
        Progress.objects.create(task=task, day=day)
    return JsonResponse({"success": True, "task_id": task.id})

@login_required
@require_POST
def toggle(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)
    task_id = int(data["task_id"])
    day = int(data["day"])
    value = bool(data["value"])
    Progress.objects.filter(task_id=task_id, day=day).update(done=value)
    return JsonResponse({"success": True})

@login_required
def get_data(request):
    tasks = Task.objects.filter(user=request.user)
    max_day = Progress.objects.filter(task__in=tasks).aggregate(models.Max("day"))["day__max"] or 0
    data = []
    for task in tasks:
        progresses = task.progresses.order_by("day")
        progress_list = [int(p.done) for p in progresses]
        data.append({"name": task.name, "id": task.id, "progress": progress_list})
    return JsonResponse({"days": max_day, "tasks": data})

@login_required
@require_POST
def add_day(request):
    tasks = Task.objects.filter(user=request.user)
    max_day = Progress.objects.filter(task__in=tasks).aggregate(models.Max("day"))["day__max"] or 0
    for task in tasks:
        Progress.objects.create(task=task, day=max_day + 1)
    return JsonResponse({"success": True})

@login_required
@require_POST
def remove_day(request):
    tasks = Task.objects.filter(user=request.user)
    max_day = Progress.objects.filter(task__in=tasks).aggregate(models.Max("day"))["day__max"] or 0
    if max_day <= 1:
        return JsonResponse({"error": "Minimum 1 day required"})
    Progress.objects.filter(task__in=tasks, day=max_day).delete()
    return JsonResponse({"success": True})

@login_required
@require_POST
def delete_task(request):
    data = json.loads(request.body)
    task_id = int(data["task_id"])
    Task.objects.filter(id=task_id, user=request.user).delete()
    return JsonResponse({"success": True})

@login_required
@require_POST
def reset_tracker(request):
    Task.objects.filter(user=request.user).delete()
    return JsonResponse({"status": "reset"})

@login_required
def chart_data(request):
    tasks = Task.objects.filter(user=request.user)
    bar = []
    line = []
    max_day = Progress.objects.filter(task__in=tasks).aggregate(models.Max("day"))["day__max"] or 0
    for task in tasks:
        completed = task.progresses.filter(done=True).count()
        total = task.progresses.count()
        percent = round((completed / total) * 100, 2) if total else 0
        bar.append({"task": task.name, "percent": percent})
    for day in range(1, max_day + 1):
        done_count = Progress.objects.filter(task__in=tasks, day=day, done=True).count()
        line.append({"day": f"Day {day}", "value": done_count})
    return JsonResponse({"bar": bar, "line": line})

@login_required
def track(request):
    return render(request, "performance.html")




import requests
from django.shortcuts import render

# 🔹 Your ngrok URL from Colab
NGROK_URL = "https://kelle-catadromous-minta.ngrok-free.dev/generate"

def recommend_view(request):
    response_text = None

    if request.method == "POST":
        topic = request.POST.get("topic")
        if topic:
            try:
                r = requests.post(NGROK_URL, json={"user_input": topic}, timeout=30)
                r.raise_for_status()
                response_text = r.json().get("response", "No response")
            except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
                response_text = f"Error: {e}"

    return render(request, "recommend.html", {"response": response_text})
